Events (SSE)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eContinuous batching of incoming requests for increased total throughput\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eA note on Shared Memory (shm)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-a-note-on-shared-memory-shm\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: A note on Shared Memory (shm)\" href=\"#a-note-on-shared-memory-shm\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/nccl/user-guide/docs/index.html\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eNCCL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e is a communication framework used by\n\u003ccode\u003ePyTorch\u003c/code\u003e to do distributed training/inference. \u003ccode\u003etext-generation-inference\u003c/code\u003e makes\nuse of \u003ccode\u003eNCCL\u003c/code\u003e to enable Tensor Parallelism to dramatically speed up inference for large language models.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn order to share data between the different devices of a \u003ccode\u003eNCCL\u003c/code\u003e group, \u003ccode\u003eNCCL\u003c/code\u003e might fall back to using the host memory if\npeer-to-peer using NVLink or PCI is not possible.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo allow the container to use 1G of Shared Memory and support SHM sharing, we add \u003ccode\u003e--shm-size 1g\u003c/code\u003e on the above command.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you are running \u003ccode\u003etext-generation-inference\u003c/code\u003e inside \u003ccode\u003eKubernetes\u003c/code\u003e. You can also add Shared Memory to the container by\ncreating a volume with:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"- name: shm\n  emptyDir:\n   medium: Memory\n   sizeLimit: 1Gi\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e- \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eshm\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eemptyDir\u003c/span\u003e:\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003emedium\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eMemory\u003c/span\u003e\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003esizeLimit\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e1Gi\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eand mounting it to \u003ccode\u003e/dev/shm\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFinally, you can also disable SHM sharing by using the \u003ccode\u003eNCCL_SHM_DISABLE=1\u003c/code\u003e environment variable. However, note that\nthis will impact performance.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDistributed Tracing\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-distributed-tracing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Distributed Tracing\" href=\"#distributed-tracing\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003etext-generation-inference\u003c/code\u003e is instrumented with distributed tracing using OpenTelemetry. dir="auto">text-generation-inference is now in maintenance mode. Going forward, we will accept pull requests for minor bug fixes, documentation improvements and lightweight maintenance tasks.</p>
<p dir="auto">TGI has initiated the movement for optimized inference engines to rely on a <code>transformers</code> model architectures. This approach is now adopted by downstream inference engines, which we contribute to and recommend using going forward: <a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm">vllm</a>, <a href="https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang">SGLang</a>, as well as local engines with inter-compatibility such as llama.cpp or MLX.</p>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Text Generation Inference</h1><a id="user-content-text-generation-inference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Text Generation Inference" href="#text-generation-inference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">A Rust, Python and gRPC server for text generation inference. Used in production at <a href="https://huggingface.co" rel="nofollow">Hugging Face</a>
to power Hugging Chat, the Inference API and Inference Endpoints.</p>
</div>

<p dir="auto">Text Generation Inference (TGI) is a toolkit for deploying and serving Large Language Models (LLMs). TGI enables high-performance text generation for the most popular open-source LLMs, including Llama, Falcon, StarCoder, BLOOM, GPT-NeoX, and <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/supported_models" rel="nofollow">more</a>. TGI implements many features, such as:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Simple launcher to serve most popular LLMs</li>
<li>Production ready (distributed tracing with Open Telemetry, Prometheus metrics)</li>
<li>Tensor Parallelism for faster inference on multiple GPUs</li>
<li>Token streaming using Server-Sent Events (SSE)</li>
<li>Continuous batching of incoming requests for increased total throughput</li>
<li><a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/en/messages_api" rel="nofollow">Messages API</a> compatible with Open AI Chat Completion API</li>
<li>Optimized transformers code for inference using <a href="https://github.com/HazyResearch/flash-attention">Flash Attention</a> and <a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm">Paged Attention</a> on the most popular architectures</li>
<li>Quantization with :
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/TimDettmers/bitsandbytes">bitsandbytes</a></li>
<li><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.17323" rel="nofollow">GPT-Q</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/NetEase-FuXi/EETQ">EETQ</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/casper-hansen/AutoAWQ">AWQ</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/IST-DASLab/marlin">Marlin</a></li>
<li><a href="https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/nvidia-arm-and-intel-publish-fp8-specification-for-standardization-as-an-interchange-format-for-ai/" rel="nofollow">fp8</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/huggingface/safetensors">Safetensors</a> weight loading</li>
<li>Watermarking with <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.10226" rel="nofollow">A Watermark for Large Language Models</a></li>
<li>Logits warper (temperature scaling, top-p, top-k, repetition penalty, more details see <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/transformers/internal/generation_utils#transformers.LogitsProcessor" rel="nofollow">transformers.LogitsProcessor</a>)</li>
<li>Stop sequences</li>
<li>Log probabilities</li>
<li><a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/conceptual/speculation" rel="nofollow">Speculation</a> ~2x latency</li>
<li><a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/conceptual/guidance" rel="nofollow">Guidance/JSON</a>. Specify output format to speed up inference and make sure the output is valid according to some specs..</li>
<li>Custom Prompt Generation: Easily generate text by providing custom prompts to guide the model's output</li>
<li>Fine-tuning Support: Utilize fine-tuned models for specific tasks to achieve higher accuracy and performance</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Hardware support</h3><a id="user-content-hardware-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hardware support" href="#hardware-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<li><a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/optimum-tpu/howto/serving" rel="nofollow">Google TPU</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Get Started</h2><a id="user-content-get-started" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Get Started" href="#get-started"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Docker</h3><a id="user-content-docker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Docker" href="#docker"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For a detailed starting guide, please see the <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/quicktour" rel="nofollow">Quick Tour</a>. The easiest way of getting started is using the official Docker container:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="model=HuggingFaceH4/zephyr-7b-beta
# share a volume with the Docker container to avoid downloading weights every run
volume=$PWD/data

docker run --gpus all --shm-size 1g -p 8080:80 -v $volume:/data \
    ghcr.io/huggingface/text-generation-inference:3.3.5 --model-id $model"><pre>model=HuggingFaceH4/zephyr-7b-beta
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> share a volume with the Docker container to avoid downloading weights every run</span>
volume=<span class="pl-smi">$PWD</span>/data

docker run --gpus all --shm-size 1g -p 8080:80 -v <span class="pl-smi">$volume</span>:/data \
    ghcr.io/huggingface/text-generation-inference:3.3.5 --model-id <span class="pl-smi">$model</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">And then you can make requests like</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl 127.0.0.1:8080/generate_stream \
    -X POST \
    -d '{&quot;inputs&quot;:&quot;What is Deep Learning?&quot;,&quot;parameters&quot;:{&quot;max_new_tokens&quot;:20}}' \
    -H 'Content-Type: application/json'"><pre>curl 127.0.0.1:8080/generate_stream \
    -X POST \
    -d <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>{"inputs":"What is Deep Learning?","parameters":{"max_new_tokens":20}}<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> \
    -H <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Content-Type: application/json<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also use <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/en/messages_api" rel="nofollow">TGI's Messages API</a> to obtain Open AI Chat Completion API compatible responses.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl localhost:8080/v1/chat/completions \
    -X POST \
    -d '{
  &quot;model&quot;: &quot;tgi&quot;,
  &quot;messages&quot;: [
    {
      &quot;role&quot;: &quot;system&quot;,
      &quot;content&quot;: &quot;You are a helpful assistant.&quot;
    },
    {
      &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
      &quot;content&quot;: &quot;What is deep learning?&quot;
    }
  ],
  &quot;stream&quot;: true,
  &quot;max_tokens&quot;: 20
}' \
    -H 'Content-Type: application/json'"><pre>curl localhost:8080/v1/chat/completions \
    -X POST \
    -d <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>{</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "model": "tgi",</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "messages": [</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {</span>
<span class="pl-s">      "role": "system",</span>
<span class="pl-s">      "content": "You are a helpful assistant."</span>
<span class="pl-s">    },</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {</span>
<span class="pl-s">      "role": "user",</span>
<span class="pl-s">      "content": "What is deep learning?"</span>
<span class="pl-s">    }</span>
<span class="pl-s">  ],</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "stream": true,</span>
<span class="pl-s">  "max_tokens": 20</span>
<span class="pl-s">}<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> \
    -H <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Content-Type: application/json<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> To use NVIDIA GPUs, you need to install the <a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/datacenter/cloud-native/container-toolkit/install-guide.html" rel="nofollow">NVIDIA Container Toolkit</a>. We also recommend using NVIDIA drivers with CUDA version 12.2 or higher. For running the Docker container on a machine with no GPUs or CUDA support, it is enough to remove the <code>--gpus all</code> flag and add <code>--disable-custom-kernels</code>, please note CPU is not the intended platform for this project, so performance might be subpar.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> TGI supports AMD Instinct MI210 and MI250 GPUs. Details can be found in the <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/installation_amd#using-tgi-with-amd-gpus" rel="nofollow">Supported Hardware documentation</a>. To use AMD GPUs, please use <code>docker run --device /dev/kfd --device /dev/dri --shm-size 1g -p 8080:80 -v $volume:/data ghcr.io/huggingface/text-generation-inference:3.3.5-rocm --model-id $model</code> instead of the command above.</p>
<p dir="auto">To see all options to serve your models (in the <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/text-generation-inference/blob/main/launcher/src/main.rs">code</a> or in the cli):</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="text-generation-launcher --help"><pre class="notranslate"><code>text-generation-launcher --help
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">API documentation</h3><a id="user-content-api-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: API documentation" href="#api-documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can consult the OpenAPI documentation of the <code>text-generation-inference</code> REST API using the <code>/docs</code> route.
The Swagger UI is also available at: <a href="https://huggingface.github.io/text-generation-inference" rel="nofollow">https://huggingface.github.io/text-generation-inference</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using a private or gated model</h3><a id="user-content-using-a-private-or-gated-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using a private or gated model" href="#using-a-private-or-gated-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You have the option to utilize the <code>HF_TOKEN</code> environment variable for configuring the token employed by
<code>text-generation-inference</code>. This allows you to gain access to protected resources.</p>
<p dir="auto">For example, if you want to serve the gated Llama V2 model variants:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Go to <a href="https://huggingface.co/settings/tokens" rel="nofollow">https://huggingface.co/settings/tokens</a></li>
<li>Copy your CLI READ token</li>
<li>Export <code>HF_TOKEN=&lt;your CLI READ token&gt;</code></li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">or with Docker:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="model=meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct
volume=$PWD/data # share a volume with the Docker container to avoid downloading weights every run
token=&lt;your cli READ token&gt;

docker run --gpus all --shm-size 1g -e HF_TOKEN=$token -p 8080:80 -v $volume:/data \
    ghcr.io/huggingface/text-generation-inference:3.3.5 --model-id $model"><pre>model=meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct
volume=<span class="pl-smi">$PWD</span>/data <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> share a volume with the Docker container to avoid downloading weights every run</span>
token=<span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>your cli READ token<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

docker run --gpus all --shm-size 1g -e HF_TOKEN=<span class="pl-smi">$token</span> -p 8080:80 -v <span class="pl-smi">$volume</span>:/data \
    ghcr.io/huggingface/text-generation-inference:3.3.5 --model-id <span class="pl-smi">$model</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">A note on Shared Memory (shm)</h3><a id="user-content-a-note-on-shared-memory-shm" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: A note on Shared Memory (shm)" href="#a-note-on-shared-memory-shm"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://docs.nvidia.com/deeplearning/nccl/user-guide/docs/index.html" rel="nofollow"><code>NCCL</code></a> is a communication framework used by
<code>PyTorch</code> to do distributed training/inference. <code>text-generation-inference</code> makes
use of <code>NCCL</code> to enable Tensor Parallelism to dramatically speed up inference for large language models.</p>
<p dir="auto">In order to share data between the different devices of a <code>NCCL</code> group, <code>NCCL</code> might fall back to using the host memory if
peer-to-peer using NVLink or PCI is not possible.</p>
<p dir="auto">To allow the container to use 1G of Shared Memory and support SHM sharing, we add <code>--shm-size 1g</code> on the above command.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you are running <code>text-generation-inference</code> inside <code>Kubernetes</code>. You can also add Shared Memory to the container by
creating a volume with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="- name: shm
  emptyDir:
   medium: Memory
   sizeLimit: 1Gi"><pre>- <span class="pl-ent">name</span>: <span class="pl-s">shm</span>
  <span class="pl-ent">emptyDir</span>:
   <span class="pl-ent">medium</span>: <span class="pl-s">Memory</span>
   <span class="pl-ent">sizeLimit</span>: <span class="pl-s">1Gi</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">and mounting it to <code>/dev/shm</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Finally, you can also disable SHM sharing by using the <code>NCCL_SHM_DISABLE=1</code> environment variable. However, note that
this will impact performance.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Distributed Tracing</h3><a id="user-content-distributed-tracing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Distributed Tracing" href="#distributed-tracing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>text-generation-inference</code> is instrumented with distributed tracing using OpenTelemetry. You can use this feature
by setting the address to an OTLP collector with the <code>--otlp-endpoint</code> argument. The default service name can be
overridden with the <code>--otlp-service-name</code> argument</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture</h3><a id="user-content-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture" href="#architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/31237363318a55d078ca2e4bc838e7bb5ed581587a5d59dd777457580f0c620e/68747470733a2f2f68756767696e67666163652e636f2f64617461736574732f68756767696e67666163652f646f63756d656e746174696f6e2d696d616765732f7265736f6c76652f6d61696e2f5447492e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/31237363318a55d078ca2e4bc838e7bb5ed581587a5d59dd777457580f0c620e/68747470733a2f2f68756767696e67666163652e636f2f64617461736574732f68756767696e67666163652f646f63756d656e746174696f6e2d696d616765732f7265736f6c76652f6d61696e2f5447492e706e67" alt="TGI architecture" data-canonical-src="https://huggingface.co/datasets/huggingface/documentation-images/resolve/main/TGI.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Detailed blogpost by Adyen on TGI inner workings: <a href="https://www.adyen.com/knowledge-hub/llm-inference-at-scale-with-tgi" rel="nofollow">LLM inference at scale with TGI (Martin Iglesias Goyanes - Adyen, 2024)</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Local install</h3><a id="user-content-local-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Local install" href="#local-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also opt to install <code>text-generation-inference</code> locally.</p>
<p dir="auto">First clone the repository and change directory into it:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/huggingface/text-generation-inference
cd text-generation-inference"><pre>git clone https://github.com/huggingface/text-generation-inference
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> text-generation-inference</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then <a href="https://rustup.rs/" rel="nofollow">install Rust</a> and create a Python virtual environment with at least
Python 3.9, e.g. using <code>conda</code> or <code>python venv</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh

#using conda
conda create -n text-generation-inference python=3.11
conda activate text-generation-inference

#using python venv
python3 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate"><pre>curl --proto <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>=https<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>using conda</span>
conda create -n text-generation-inference python=3.11
conda activate text-generation-inference

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>using python venv</span>
python3 -m venv .venv
<span class="pl-c1">source</span> .venv/bin/activate</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You may also need to install Protoc.</p>
<p dir="auto">On Linux:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="PROTOC_ZIP=protoc-21.12-linux-x86_64.zip
curl -OL https://github.com/protocolbuffers/protobuf/releases/download/v21.12/$PROTOC_ZIP
sudo unzip -o $PROTOC_ZIP -d /usr/local bin/protoc
sudo unzip -o $PROTOC_ZIP -d /usr/local 'include/*'
rm -f $PROTOC_ZIP"><pre>PROTOC_ZIP=protoc-21.12-linux-x86_64.zip
curl -OL https://github.com/protocolbuffers/protobuf/releases/download/v21.12/<span class="pl-smi">$PROTOC_ZIP</span>
sudo unzip -o <span class="pl-smi">$PROTOC_ZIP</span> -d /usr/local bin/protoc
sudo unzip -o <span class="pl-smi">$PROTOC_ZIP</span> -d /usr/local <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>include/*<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>
rm -f <span class="pl-smi">$PROTOC_ZIP</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">On MacOS, using Homebrew:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="brew install protobuf"><pre>brew install protobuf</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="BUILD_EXTENSIONS=True make install # Install repository and HF/transformer fork with CUDA kernels
text-generation-launcher --model-id mistralai/Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2"><pre>BUILD_EXTENSIONS=True make install <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Install repository and HF/transformer fork with CUDA kernels</span>
text-generation-launcher --model-id mistralai/Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> on some machines, you may also need the OpenSSL libraries and gcc. On Linux machines, run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sudo apt-get install libssl-dev gcc -y"><pre>sudo apt-get install libssl-dev gcc -y</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Local install (Nix)</h3><a id="user-content-local-install-nix" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Local install (Nix)" href="#local-install-nix"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Another option is to install <code>text-generation-inference</code> locally using <a href="https://nixos.org" rel="nofollow">Nix</a>. Currently,
we only support Nix on x86_64 Linux with CUDA GPUs. When using Nix, all dependencies can
be pulled from a binary cache, removing the need to build them locally.</p>
<p dir="auto">First follow the instructions to <a href="https://app.cachix.org/cache/huggingface" rel="nofollow">install Cachix and enable the Hugging Face cache</a>.
Setting up the cache is important, otherwise Nix will build many of the dependencies
locally, which can take hours.</p>
<p dir="auto">After that you can run TGI with <code>nix run</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd text-generation-inference
nix run --extra-experimental-features nix-command --extra-experimental-features flakes . -- --model-id meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> text-generation-inference
nix run --extra-experimental-features nix-command --extra-experimental-features flakes <span class="pl-c1">.</span> -- --model-id meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> when you are using Nix on a non-NixOS system, you have to <a href="https://danieldk.eu/Nix-CUDA-on-non-NixOS-systems#make-runopengl-driverlib-and-symlink-the-driver-library" rel="nofollow">make some symlinks</a>
to make the CUDA driver libraries visible to Nix packages.</p>
<p dir="auto">For TGI development, you can use the <code>impure</code> dev shell:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="nix develop .#impure

# Only needed the first time the devshell is started or after updating the protobuf.
(
cd server
mkdir text_generation_server/pb || true
python -m grpc_tools.protoc -I../proto/v3 --python_out=text_generation_server/pb \
       --grpc_python_out=text_generation_server/pb --mypy_out=text_generation_server/pb ../proto/v3/generate.proto
find text_generation_server/pb/ -type f -name &quot;*.py&quot; -print0 -exec sed -i -e 's/^\(import.*pb2\)/from . \1/g' {} \;
touch text_generation_server/pb/__init__.py
)"><pre>nix develop .<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>impure</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Only needed the first time the devshell is started or after updating the protobuf.</span>
(
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> server
mkdir text_generation_server/pb <span class="pl-k">||</span> <span class="pl-c1">true</span>
python -m grpc_tools.protoc -I../proto/v3 --python_out=text_generation_server/pb \
       --grpc_python_out=text_generation_server/pb --mypy_out=text_generation_server/pb ../proto/v3/generate.proto
find text_generation_server/pb/ -type f -name <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>*.py<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> -print0 -exec sed -i -e <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>s/^\(import.*pb2\)/from . \1/g<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> {} <span class="pl-cce">\;</span>
touch text_generation_server/pb/__init__.py
)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">All development dependencies (cargo, Python, Torch), etc. are available in this
dev shell.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Optimized architectures</h2><a id="user-content-optimized-architectures" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Optimized architectures" href="#optimized-architectures"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">TGI works out of the box to serve optimized models for all modern models. They can be found in <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/supported_models" rel="nofollow">this list</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Other architectures are supported on a best-effort basis using:</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(&lt;model&gt;, device_map="auto")</code></p>
<p dir="auto">or</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>AutoModelForSeq2SeqLM.from_pretrained(&lt;model&gt;, device_map="auto")</code></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run locally</h2><a id="user-content-run-locally" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run locally" href="#run-locally"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run</h3><a id="user-content-run" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run" href="#run"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="text-generation-launcher --model-id mistralai/Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2"><pre>text-generation-launcher --model-id mistralai/Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quantization</h3><a id="user-content-quantization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quantization" href="#quantization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can also run pre-quantized weights (AWQ, GPTQ, Marlin) or on-the-fly quantize weights with bitsandbytes, EETQ, fp8, to reduce the VRAM requirement:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="text-generation-launcher --model-id mistralai/Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 --quantize"><pre>text-generation-launcher --model-id mistralai/Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 --quantize</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">4bit quantization is available using the <a href="https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.14314.pdf" rel="nofollow">NF4 and FP4 data types from bitsandbytes</a>. It can be enabled by providing <code>--quantize bitsandbytes-nf4</code> or <code>--quantize bitsandbytes-fp4</code> as a command line argument to <code>text-generation-launcher</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Read more about quantization in the <a href="https://huggingface.co/docs/text-generation-inference/en/conceptual/quantization" rel="nofollow">Quantization documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Develop</h2><a id="user-content-develop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Develop" href="#develop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make server-dev
make router-dev"><pre>make server-dev
make router-dev</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Testing</h2><a id="user-content-testing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Testing" href="#testing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
